- `2026_03_TEN_NYG::NYG_SIDE` (same, strong (qualitative)): NYG moneyline, NYG spread, NYG team total over, NYG player props over — Team-side markets share the same game-script risk.
- `2026_03_TEN_NYG::GAME_SCRIPT_OPPOSED` (opposed, moderate (qualitative)): favourite rushing volume over, favourite passing volume over — A team that leads runs more and throws less; volume props on the same team can conflict.
